    Board and/or the Committee may amend other terms of a Director&rsquo;s grant(s) to the extent provided in the applicable Equity
    Plan and subject to Applicable Law.</FONT></TD></TR>
</TABLE>
<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&nbsp;</FONT></P>

<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%">

    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; width: 29px; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">6.3.</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><U>Expenses</U></FONT></TD></TR>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0 0pt 53px; text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">All
Directors may be reimbursed for their reasonable expenses (against invoices) incurred in connection with attending meetings of
the Board and committee&rsquo;s thereof (including domestic and international travel expenses) and travelling on behalf of the
Company, consistent with the Company&rsquo;s practices and policies.</FONT></P>

    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; width: 29px; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">6.4.</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><U>Insurance,
    Exculpation and Indemnification</U></FONT></TD></TR>
</TABLE>
<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&nbsp;</FONT></P>

<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%">
<TR STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; width: 53px; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; width: 48px; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">6.4.1.</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
    Company may indemnify, exculpate and insure Directors (including in their capacity as directors of the Company&rsquo;s subsidiaries)
    to the fullest extent permitted by Applicable Law from time to time.</FONT></TD></TR>
</TABLE>
<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&nbsp;</FONT></P>

<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%">
<TR STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; width: 53px; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; width: 48px; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">6.4.2.</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Subject
    to Applicable Law, the acquisition, extension, renewal or replacement of directors&rsquo; and officers&rsquo; liability insurance
    (&ldquo;<B>D&amp;O Insurance</B>&rdquo;) may be approved solely by the Committee provided that (i) the maximum aggregate limit
    of liability pursuant to the D&amp;O Insurance (including Side &ldquo;A&rdquo; coverage) shall be not more than US$50,000,000
    (fifty million U.S. Dollars) for each D&amp;O Insurance period; and (iv) the D&amp;O Insurance is on market terms and shall
    not have a material impact on the Company&rsquo;s profitability, assets or liabilities.</FONT></TD></TR>
</TABLE>
<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&nbsp;</FONT></P>

<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%; background-color: white">
<TR STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; width: 24px; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">7.</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: small-caps"><B><U>Recoupment</U></B></FONT></TD></TR>
</TABLE>
<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0 0pt 0.25in; text-align: justify; background-color: white">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0 0pt 0.25in; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Terms of Office and Engagement of a Director shall include provisions that require a Director to repay to the Company amounts
paid to such Directors as part of the Terms of Office and Engagement, if they were paid on the basis of figures that later transpire
to be incorrect and were restated in the Company&rsquo;s financial statements. The Compensation Committee shall be entitled to
determine the amounts and conditions of such repayment, which may include terms under which (i) repayment will be made either
on a pre-tax basis or an after-tax basis, unless and to the extent the Director was able to recoup tax payments made with respect
to the amounts to be repaid, (ii) no repayment obligation shall arise after the lapse of a period of time of no less than three
years from the date on which the original payment was made, (iii) the period of time of no more than 12 months over which the
repayment payments to the Company shall be made and the ability to make the repayment in installments or (to the extent permitted
under Applicable Law) as a set-off against cash compensation paid by the Company to the Director during such period , (iv) no
repayment obligation shall arise in the event that the reason or basis for the restatement was due to changes in the Applicable
Law, including generally acceptable accounting principles or financial reporting standards; and (v) such other provisions as determined
in each case, subject to Applicable Law. Nothing in this Section <FONT STYLE="background-color: aqua">&lrm;</FONT>7 shall derogate
from or limit any other or similar provisions imposed on a Director by Applicable Law, including, securities laws.</FONT></P>

<P STYLE="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0">&nbsp;</P>

<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%; background-color: white">
<TR STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <TD STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0; width: 24px; text-indent: 0"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">8.</FONT></TD>
